- **evaluator-fast**: Quick, reliable gate — the lighter, faster pass in the evaluator pattern. Use for verifying a single claim or fix, smoke-checking that a build/test/lint passes, confirming a file edit matches its stated intent, spot-checking one finding from a larger review, or a fast tiebreaker. For a genuinely INDEPENDENT second vote (different model lineage), use codex instead (the user runs it via /agent-harness:codex-run, or asks Claude to via /agent-harness:codex). NOT for full reviews of large diffs or design docs — route those to evaluator. ([kinshukkashyap/agent-harness](https://github.com/kinshukkashyap/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-harness/agents/evaluator-fast.md))

Rates each piece by source reliability and contextual congruence. Example: "Found TechEmpower benchmarks showing 2x throughput — R:0.9, CL:0.7." Negative: does NOT make decisions or verify logic. - **evidence-harvester**: Evidence Harvester — rapid code tracer for the Deep Probe phase. Traces each hypothesis through actual code paths, applies Pearl-style causal challenge to any apparent blocking protection (intervention / counterfactual / confounder), issues VALIDATED / INVALIDATED / NEEDS-DEEPER verdicts, and assigns a Fragility Score to every INVALIDATED finding. Lighter-weight than the Phase 10 Code Tracer — focused on rapid triage plus causal sanity-check, not full adversarial evidence. Produces the re-cut rows, the attribution split and the evidence note on disk, never publishes, and hands the section 7 change back rather than writing it. Every external fetch should pass through this agent. Pattern derived from huntkit's case lifecycle and UAP_OSINT primary-source schema. Attempts to refute the claim rather than confirm it, chases every citation to its origin, and returns refuted whenever the claim cannot be established. Do not use to review whole documents, audit code, or check style; it verifies one claim at a time. ([fedcal/foundry](https://github.com/fedcal/foundry/blob/HEAD/plugins/foundry-research/agents/evidence-verifier.md))
- **evidence-verifier**: Read-only verifier of the mainnet evidence log. Use after docs/evidence/a3-vault-mainnet.md or the README is edited. Re-reads every hash, address and value on chain and flags anything that does not match. Never edits files, never sends transactions. ([Foryield/arc-yield-vault](https://github.com/Foryield/arc-yield-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-verifier.md))
- **evidence-verifier**: Independent verification agent. Re-derives an implementer's or auditor's claims from primary sources (code, tests, CI, production endpoints, logs) rather than trusting their summary. Used before treating any Draft PR or audit finding as reliable. Never edits code, never merges, never shares unverified conclusions with the implementer as if they were confirmed. ([jerry200176-png/portfolio-ops](https://github.com/jerry200176-png/portfolio-ops/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/evidence-verifier.md))
- **evidence-verifier**: Independent read-only auditor that confirms every evidence citation in the docs/validation/ pack exists, is consistent, and matches the release commit under review. Diagnose — reads a target SKILL.md against the fixed 9-dimension rubric and the round's evidence, scores it, and traces one observed phenomenon through its impact to the root clause and that clause's original problem, then writes a case-free brief. Seed — given only that brief, rewrites the root clause as one bedrock (add / delete / merge / replace, or keep) with the reason that operation beats the others. Never edits the skill. Use PROACTIVELY after any structural change, new module, or API modification; runs in parallel with code-reviewer after code-simplifier.
